- **r-start**: Reload context and display project health. This is the recommended first command in any session. It gives the agent a warm handoff — enough context to continue where the last session left off. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-start.md) · ★20)

- **r-util-chg-dedup**: Deduplicate changelog entries for the current version. Scans CHANGELOG and CHANGELOG.RELEASE for duplicates, overlapping entries, tracking artifacts, fluff, and section proliferation. Report mode by default — only modify files when explicitly told to fix. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-chg-dedup.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-chg-gen**: Generate changelog entries from the current diff or commit range. Read governance for changelog format rules. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-chg-gen.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-ci-gen**: Generate a GitHub Actions CI workflow from the project's test infrastructure. Creates workflow files for user review — does NOT commit. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-ci-gen.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-claudemd-review**: Analyze recent conversations to find improvements for CLAUDE.md files, and audit existing CLAUDE.md content for prose drift and memory-bound state contamination. Read-only report — no modifications. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-claudemd-review.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-code-map**: Structural map of a source file — classes, functions, methods with line ranges. Reduces context cost when an agent needs one symbol from a large file by letting it read only the relevant line range. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-code-map.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-code-modernize**: Assess codebase modernization maturity and generate a phased remediation plan. This is a **read-only assessment** — it does NOT modify any project files. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-code-modernize.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-code-scan**: Structured pattern-class bug finder. Searches project files for instances of known anti-patterns or a custom pattern. Groups results by file and function, cross-references AUDIT.md for tracked instances. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-code-scan.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-doc-gen**: Generate publication-ready documentation for a specific surface of the current project. Reads existing content, applies project documentation standards from governance, ensures cross-surface consistency, and presents results for user review before applying changes. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-doc-gen.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-lib-release**: Shared library release lifecycle. Handles pre-flight checks, release preparation, and consumer drift notification. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-lib-release.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-lib-sync**: Cross-project shared library drift detection. Compares canonical library sources against all consumer copies. Report mode by default; optional sync mode copies and verifies. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-lib-sync.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-mem-audit**: Stale memory and contradiction detection. Reads MEMORY.md files and cross-references every factual claim against live project state. Read-only report — no modifications. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-mem-audit.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-mem-compact**: Archive stale MEMORY.md entries. Detects staleness, identifies completed work ready for archival, and proposes compaction. Preview mode by default — only modifies files when user explicitly confirms. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-mem-compact.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-proj-cross**: Cross-project analysis for workspace project sets. Reads MEMORY.md, PLAN.md, AUDIT.md, and CLAUDE.md from all projects to identify shared patterns, duplicate effort, convention drift, and alignment opportunities. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-proj-cross.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-rel-squash**: Generate a squash plan for the current release branch by grouping commits into logical buckets aligned with CHANGELOG.RELEASE entries. Default: plan summary only. - **r-util-test-dedup**: Find duplicate, overlapping, tautological, dead, and low-value tests in the project's test suite. Read-only static analysis — does not modify any files or execute the test suite. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-test-dedup.md) · ★20)
- **r-util-test-scope**: Analyze the current diff to recommend which tests to run and map changed functions to their test files. Combines test tier selection with function-to-test impact analysis. - **read-file-at**: Read a file's **bytes as of a revision** — the structured equivalent of `git show &lt;rev&gt;:&lt;path&gt;` / `git cat-file blob &lt;rev&gt;:&lt;path&gt;`. - **readers**: List the Personas you can simulate against before spending on a Simulation ([unpitch/unpitch-mcp](https://github.com/unpitch/unpitch-mcp/blob/HEAD/commands/readers.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **readfile**: - Use this page as the authoritative reference for **Readfile**. - Follow the official Cypress docs for supported APIs and patterns. - Keep examples aligned with the section (App / API). ([full-stack-skills/testing-skills](https://github.com/full-stack-skills/testing-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/cypress/api/commands/readfile.md) · ★2 · 21 copies)
- **readiness**: Phase 0. Check if the buyer is ready to acquire, and set the criteria for a target. ([ai-advanced-futures/cc-plugins](https://github.com/ai-advanced-futures/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/corporate-development/commands/readiness.md))
- **readiness**: An honest, bounded answer to "am I ready for this course?" — evidence, weakest items and caveats; never a grade prediction ([alwayslistening86-pixel/edu-course-library](https://github.com/alwayslistening86-pixel/edu-course-library/blob/HEAD/plugin/generic-tutor/commands/readiness.md))
- **readiness**: Run the pre-launch engineering pass — launch readiness and technical-claim review ([blakebauman/vital](https://github.com/blakebauman/vital/blob/HEAD/commands/readiness.md))
- **readiness**: Quick self-check of a team's readiness to run with AI agents, scored against the bundled 6-area rubric; writes agent-readiness.md ([fmolinoferrer/felipe-plugins](https://github.com/fmolinoferrer/felipe-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-ops/commands/readiness.md))
